So I'm just doing it all at once.\n\nDoctor: For how many days in a row have you been feeling this unusually energized and driven?\n\nPatient: I'd say... well, it's been building for about a month now. I don't know if I would have ended up feeling this way regardless. But at the same time, I'd get suddenly furious if anyone questioned me. Even over really minor things, like small edits to a document or just routine budget questions. I just... I had absolutely no patience for anyone slowing me down.\n\nDoctor: During these past few weeks, have you been needing much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say I've needed less sleep. I mean, I haven't really noticed a change in how much I'm sleeping, to be honest. I usually get around the same amount every night, and that hasn't seemed any different lately.\n\nDoctor: During this time, have you had experiences like hearing or seeing things other people couldn't, or beliefs that others said were not true?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I haven't heard any voices or seen anything that wasn't there. And I don't think I've had any strange beliefs, really... just that I felt really strongly about my ideas for work. But nobody told me I was out of touch with reality or anything, just that I was moving too fast and bypassing the rules.\n\nDoctor: Besides the problems at work, has this period caused serious problems in other parts of your life?\n\nPatient: Well, the work stuff has definitely been the biggest issue. Because of how I handled the launch and... and all the arguments that came with it, I've actually been put on administrative leave. So, yeah, that's a pretty serious problem. They basically told me I'm no longer trusted to manage the HR projects I was leading. It's... it's a huge mess. But outside of work, no, I haven't really noticed any major problems in my personal life or anything like that. It's mostly just my job that's been affected.\n\nDoctor: During the past few weeks, have you been more driven than usual to start or push through projects or other activities?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I definitely have. I've been trying to do a lot all at once. I was running the HR redesign at work, but outside of that, I was also trying to line up gallery partners, contact festival organizers, and fix several staff conflicts all at the exact same time. It just felt like... well, like every single idea I had needed to be acted on immediately.\n\nDoctor: During this period, did you feel more important, powerful, or unusually confident than you normally do?\n\nPatient: Um, no, not really. I didn't feel more powerful or important than usual. I mean, I was confident in the plans I was pushing at work, but... just normal confidence, I think. I didn't feel like I was invincible or anything like that, just really focused on getting all those projects done.\n\nDoctor: During this period, were you talking more than usual or feeling like you couldn't stop talking?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. I've noticed that in meetings, I... well, I can actually hear myself talking faster and a lot longer than usual. I tend to just jump from topic to topic—like going straight from employee engagement to the gallery layouts, and then right into retention metrics.
